# DESIGNER PRESTIGE EIGHTEEN — 3,000–15,000 THB Thai Department-Store Mass

**Status:** QUARANTINED — historical analysis only; do not mix or release.
**Artifact review (2026-10-05):** The stored analysis no longer matches the current inventory, scientific inputs or implementation. Formula quantities and prior analysis are retained; no fresh calculation, sensory validation or safety/release approval is claimed. See the [review receipt](../../docs/verification/FORMULA_ARTIFACT_REVIEW_20261005.json).

**Date:** 2026-05-02
**Format:** 18 × 30 mL EDP / Cologne Absolue — Thai mass-prestige tier (Central, Siam Paragon, EmQuartier, ICONSIAM, King Power, Eveandboy upper). 3,000–15,000 THB / 100 mL retail equivalent (~$80–400 USD).
**Tier definition:** the actual designer flagships sold at Thai department-store fragrance counters. NOT drugstore (Mistine/Cathy Doll), NOT niche-luxury (Roja/Amouage/Frédéric Malle, 25,000+ THB), NOT extreme niche (Maison Francis Kurkdjian Baccarat Rouge 540, 18,000+ THB).
**Gender split:** 9 women / unisex + 9 masculine.
**What this tier actually is in Thailand:**
- LVMH (Dior, Givenchy, Guerlain, Kenzo)
- L'Oréal Luxe (YSL, Lancôme, Armani, Mugler, Viktor & Rolf)
- Coty Prestige (Chloé, Hugo Boss, Calvin Klein, Tiffany, Gucci)
- Estée Lauder Companies (Tom Ford signature line, Estée Lauder, Aerin, Clinique Happy)
- Chanel (Coco Mademoiselle, Bleu, Allure, Chance, N°5)
- Hermès (Terre, Twilly, H24)
- Dolce & Gabbana, Versace, Prada, Bvlgari (Coty-licensed)
- Niche-prestige edge: Le Labo, Jo Malone, Diptyque, Maison Margiela Replica (mostly 7,000–12,000 THB tier in Thailand)

Maple Lactone 20 % at 350 µL = 0.23 % active builds the praline-caramel facet. Patchouli 350 + Vanillin 1.7 % + Tonka FO + Coumarin = the signature LVEB iso-e-super-Cashmeran-vanilla cocoon. Hedione total 730 µL ≈ 11 % of concentrate gives the radiant transparency.

**Signature:** ~32 % of concentrate is citrus oils — Cologne Absolue territory (much heavier than standard 4-6 % cologne). Blood Orange Sicilian 300 µL is the Atelier Orange Sanguine signature. Heart is herbal-light (Petitgrain + Neroli + Lavender) per AdP Colonia structure. Base intentionally transparent — even at 18 %, this dries down in 4–5 hr, true cologne family.

## 18. STORM WAVE — Issey Miyake L'Eau d'Issey Pour Homme direction

**Gender:** M. **Tier:** 4,000–5,500 THB / 100 mL.
**Reference profile:** L'Eau d'Issey Pour Homme (1994) — yuzu, bergamot, lemon, sage, lily of the valley, water lily, cinnamon, vetiver, sandalwood, tobacco, musks. Pioneer of modern men's marine-ozonic.
**Concentrate:** 20 % EDP, 6,000 µL.

**Signature:** Citrus blend reconstructs yuzu (Bergamot + Cedrat + Citral + Lime + Limonene gives the yuzu GC headspace). Lily-water-lily heart from Mayol + Lilyreal + Helional. Cinnamaldehyde 14 µL = 0.047 % stays under Cat 4 0.05 %. Tobacco FTEC trace at base = Issey's distinctive dry-tobacco closer. Iso E Super 500 µL = the sleek-modern radiance Issey pioneered.

## Material cost framing (Thai retail material cost / 30 mL)

| Build | Material cost ~ THB / 30 mL | Retail tier match |
|---|---|---|
| Belle Réverie | ~180 | LVEB-class 4,500–6,000 |
| White Bloom | ~250 (ylang heavy + champaca) | J'adore-class 5,500–7,500 |
| Velvet Powder | ~200 | Trésor/Idôle 4,500–6,500 |
| Midnight Coffee | ~170 | Black Opium 5,500–7,000 |
| Crimson Velvet | ~190 | Hypnotic Poison 4,500–6,000 |
| Black Vanille | ~280 (heavy tobacco FTEC + Tobacco Fleuressence + 24 % concentration) | TF Tobacco Vanille 10,000–13,000 |
| Cobalt | ~220 (heavy Ambrox + sandalwood stack) | Bleu de Chanel 6,000–8,000 |
| Cedar Smoke | ~280 (Polysantol + Javanol premium sandalwood stack) | Le Labo Santal 33 9,000–12,000 |
| Whispered Skin | ~190 | Cashmere Mist / Burberry Her 5,000–7,000 |
| Demoiselle | ~210 (heavy patchouli + Hedione 14 %) | Coco Mademoiselle 5,500–7,500 |
| Romance Bouquet | ~200 | Mon Guerlain 5,000–6,500 |
| Patchouli Absolu | ~300 (patchouli 800 µL + 5-resin stack + premium sandalwood) | TF Patchouli Absolu 10,000–14,000 |
| Gentleman | ~210 | Givenchy Gentleman 5,500–7,500 |
| Eros | ~180 | Versace Eros 4,500–6,000 |
| Legend | ~190 | Le Male EDP 4,000–5,500 |
| Marine Profumo | ~210 (olibanum + frankincense + heavy Iso E) | AdG Profumo 5,500–7,500 |
| Coloniale | ~190 (heavy citrus oils — Sicilian-grade) | Atelier Cologne 6,000–9,000 |
| Storm Wave | ~190 | Issey Pour Homme 4,000–5,500 |

**Average:** ~215 THB material cost / 30 mL. Retail tier ratio: material cost / retail ≈ 4–5 % — true designer-prestige economics (vs. 12–15 % for Thai mass-tier, vs. 1–3 % for niche-luxury where most cost is brand/packaging).

## Compounding & maceration

- **Standard prestige EDP (16 builds):** 6 weeks maceration, weekly inversion, store cool-dark.
- **TF Private Blend tier (Black Vanille, Patchouli Absolu):** 8 weeks — heavy resin / oud stand-in / patchouli need maximum integration.
- **Cologne Absolue (Coloniale):** 3 weeks — citrus-dominant, fast integration.
- **Aquatic-fougère (Eros, Storm Wave, Marine Profumo, Cobalt):** 4 weeks — Calone / Floralozone are stable; Ambroxan-heavy needs only moderate maturation.

## What this collection is and is not

**This collection IS:**
- Direct-dupe formulas of 18 actual Thai dept-store prestige flagships (not Thai-localized, not body-mist tier)
- Engineered for the 3,000–15,000 THB / 100 mL retail tier where most Thai prestige fragrance volume actually moves
- Half women / unisex (9), half masculine (9)
- Mostly EDP at 18–24 % concentration; one Cologne Absolue at 18 %
- Built entirely from current inventory (no new materials needed)

**This collection is NOT:**
- Niche-luxury (Roja, Amouage, Frédéric Malle, Maison Francis Kurkdjian — 25,000+ THB tier)
- Thai-localized (no champaca, lotus, pandan, lemongrass naturals leading the formulas — those went into v1)
- Drugstore mass (Mistine, Karmart, Twelve Plus body mists — those went into v2)
- Original / experimental compositions (the project's iris-jasmine and Opus V niche builds serve that purpose)
